dipoles are spaced )',/4 wavelength apart and are fed 900 out of phase, result-
ing in а unidirectional pattern. А bidirectional (two-way) pattern can Ье
obtained Ьу spacing the dipoles 1.0).- apart and feeding them in phase, as
shown at В.
